Per-record provenance pages

Every record page links to its provenance page under review/<record> (348 pages): the status of the index row and of the digitized points, the reconciliation of the two readings, the calibration residuals, the as-printed values and the two agents’ notes. The record pages themselves carry only what a user of the data needs.

Cells that one agent supplied and nobody checked
